### [1] [IaC-Guard-V: A Verification Framework for LLM-Generated Infrastructure-as-Code Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28488)
*Lokesh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出以验证为中心的框架 IaC-Guard-V，从语法有效性、目标问题修复、回归安全性和补丁最小性四个维度评估 LLM 生成的 IaC 修复；在 630 次运行中发现单轮修复全验证通过率仅 32-50%，验证引导的迭代修复可提升至 68-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IaC 配置错误是云安全事件的主要诱因，LLM 被越来越多地用来自动修复，但其生成修复的可信度缺乏研究。IaC 面临独特验证挑战：不同扫描工具与配置行为不一致、基础设施语义无法用常规单元测试验证、厂商特定规则导致验证环境碎片化。

Method: 提出 IaC-Guard-V 验证中心框架，按四个维度评估 AI 生成的 IaC 修复：语法有效性、目标问题解决、回归安全性、补丁最小性。构建包含 70 个真实错误配置的 Terraform 与 Kubernetes 工件基准，覆盖 70 条唯一扫描器规则和 8 类违规；在三个 LLM 家族、三种修复策略下进行 630 次运行。

Result: 所有模型语法有效率达 100%，但单轮修复仅 32-50% 通过完整验证；验证引导的迭代修复将验证通过率显著提升至 68-92%。开源模型配合验证引导的修复，其表现优于最强商用模型的非验证模式，且每次验证修复成本仅为其十二分之一。商用模型对 Kubernetes 修复接近完美，而开源模型需依赖验证引导迭代。结构化提示持续降低 Terraform 修复质量。

Conclusion: LLM 生成 IaC 修复的可信度不能仅凭语法正确判断，验证引导的迭代修复是关键；结构化提示对 Terraform 修复反而有害，挑战了关于约束 LLM 输出的常见假设。基准与工件已开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) misconfigurations are a leading cause of cloud security incidents, and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as automated repair agents. Yet the trustworthiness of LLM-generated IaC repairs remains underexplored. IaC presents distinctive verification challenges: security scanners may behave differently across tools and configurations, infrastructure semantics cannot be validated through ordinary unit tests, and provider-specific rules create a fragmented verification landscape. We present IaC-Guard-V, a verification-centered framework that evaluates AI-generated IaC repairs through four dimensions: syntactic validity, target-issue resolution, regression safety, and patch minimality. We construct a benchmark of 70 real-world misconfigured Terraform and Kubernetes artifacts spanning 70 unique scanner rules and eight violation classes, and evaluate three repair strategies across three LLM families in 630 runs. Although all models achieve 100% syntactic validity, only 32-50% of single-shot repairs pass full verification. Verification-guided iterative repair significantly improves verified-fix rates to 68-92%. An open-source model with verification-guided repair outperforms the strongest commercial model without verification at one-twelfth the cost per verified fix. Kubernetes repairs reach near-perfect rates for commercial models but require verification-guided iteration for the open-source model. Structured prompting consistently reduces Terraform repair quality, challenging common assumptions about constrained LLM output. The benchmark and artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [2] [Pretraining and adapting a language model on a dependency-free stack: GPT-2 124M from random weights, reproduced against llm.c, and a clinical adapter for Qwen3-0.6B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28568)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 用 Zig 编写的零依赖 ML 栈 numbat 独立完成了 GPT-2（124.4M）预训练与临床问答适配的完整生命周期，与 PyTorch 参考实现结果接近，且吞吐略高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前几乎所有在役语言模型都由同一族软件训练，这种单一实现的高度集中使得一个根本问题难以回答：关于语言模型训练的既有知识中，有多少刻画的是语言模型本身，有多少只是在刻画那套软件。要厘清该问题，需要第二个独立实现，且必须能贯穿模型完整生命周期，而非仅复现单个算子。

Method: 使用 numbat——一套以 Zig 编写、不含任何第三方运行时依赖的机器学习栈——从随机初始化出发，在 9.91B token 的网页文本上预训练 124.4M 参数的 GPT-2；随后将一个独立小模型适配到临床问答任务。两个阶段均在相同硬件上并行运行参考实现，并由一个有权中止运行的 sidecar 对每次运行进行监督。

Result: 一致性较高：留出集交叉熵收敛于 3.2588（已发表值 3.29），HellaSwag 为 0.3053（对比 0.299）；在 8 组配对评测中，numbat 每一点均低于同机参考实现，平均低 0.0608。将参考实现换到不同硬件重跑会产生 0.0035 的漂移，这为区分“方法差异”与“框架差异”提供了上界。吞吐未因一致性而牺牲：单次会话中在生产配置下 numbat 达 43,374 tokens/s，PyTorch 为 41,202，三卡扩展比为 2.769x。临床适配最终留出损失为 2.1899，参考为 2.1941。

Conclusion: 以 numbat 为代表的第二套独立实现能够贯穿语言模型从预训练到下游适配的完整生命周期，并在精度与吞吐上均与主流通用框架相当，说明关于训练的相当一部分知识并非该特定软件的产物。两个模型均非医疗器械，也未针对临床使用进行验证。

Abstract: Almost every language model in service was trained by one family of software. That concentration makes a question hard to settle: how much of what is known about training a language model describes language models, and how much describes that software? Settling it needs a second implementation able to carry a model through a whole lifecycle rather than reproduce one operator.
  We report such a lifecycle. Using numbat, a machine-learning stack written in Zig with no third-party runtime dependencies, we pretrain a 124.4 M-parameter GPT-2 from random initialisation over 9.91 B tokens of web text, then adapt a separate small model to clinical question answering. A reference implementation runs on identical hardware at both stages, and a sidecar with authority to halt a run supervises each.
  Agreement is close. Held-out cross-entropy finishes at 3.2588 against a published 3.29, and HellaSwag at 0.3053 against 0.299; across 8 paired evaluations it sits below a same-machine reference at every point, by 0.0608 on average. Re-running that reference on different hardware moves it 0.0035, which bounds how much of any gap is method rather than framework. Throughput does not pay for agreement: measured in one session at a production configuration, numbat reaches 43,374 tokens per second against PyTorch's 41,202, scaling 2.769x over three cards. Clinical adaptation ends at 2.1899 held-out loss against 2.1941.
  Neither model is a medical device, and neither is validated for clinical use.

</details>


### [3] [Change-Provenant Supervision: Governing Learned Artifacts Under Policy Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28574)
*Jesus Salas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 记录型依赖图无法自证无遗漏边，故图范围内的失效判定不足以在权威变更后准入已学习工件。作者将"记录谱系"（提出影响范围与依赖解释）与"独立按当前契约重验证"分离，并分两层证据评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录型依赖图存在两个根本缺口：(1) 无法证明图中没有遗漏的边；(2) 输出测试可能漏检"谱系已过期"的派生结果。因此单靠图范围的失效判定无法在权威/契约变更后为工件准入提供保障，需要引入独立于记录谱系的有效性依据。

Method: 设计上将"记录谱系"（仅用于提出影响范围与依赖解释）与"对每个保留目标的独立当前契约重验证"（提供相对于声明契约的可靠性）相分离。评估分两层证据：(1) 四个 Qwen3-14B LoRA 包，将 1.028 GB 适配器字节绑定到训练与权威记录，比较陈旧/全新鲜/朴素追加/谱系选择四类包，采用字节派生重建与封存的当前契约账本进行准入判定；(2) 受控试验台，含六个角色分离的修正案，对比传递谱系与文档级失效的召回与提名规模，并做遗漏承重边的消融。

Result: 80 个权威中性输入上，陈旧与全新鲜适配器输出完全相同的计划，故输出测试无法区分二者；但字节派生重建与封存契约账本拒绝了陈旧包和朴素追加包，准入全新鲜包与谱系选择包。受控试验台中，传递谱系找回了全部 40 个语义上失效的"修正案-目标"对，同时提名 56 个影响候选，而文档级失效为 120 个；56 个候选均有到被修正条款的已声明路径，其中 16 个内容仍然有效。每个修正案省略一条承重边使语义召回降至 34/40，同时独立重验证拒绝了全部六个受影响包。

Conclusion: 结果确立的是一个受控治理机制的性质，而非企业级失效比例、遗忘效果、操作最优流程或可测的总成本降低。

Abstract: A recorded dependency graph cannot certify that it contains no omitted edge. For learned artifacts, graph-scoped invalidation therefore cannot by itself justify admission after authority changes, especially when output testing misses a provenance-stale derivation. We separate recorded lineage, which proposes impact scope and a dependency explanation, from independent current-contract revalidation of every retained target, which supplies soundness relative to the declared contract.
  We evaluate this design in two evidence layers. Four Qwen3-14B LoRA packages bind 1.028 GB of adapter bytes to training and authority records. Stale and full-fresh adapters trained on mutually exclusive versioned supervision emitted identical plans on all 80 tested authority-neutral inputs. Byte-derived reconstruction and a sealed current-contract ledger nevertheless refused the stale and naive-append packages and admitted full-fresh and lineage-selective packages. In a controlled harness with six role-separated amendments, transitive lineage recovered all 40 semantically invalidated amendment-target pairs while nominating 56 impact candidates, compared with 120 under document-wide invalidation. All 56 had a declared path to an amended clause, although 16 remained content-valid. Omitting one load-bearing edge per amendment reduced semantic recall to 34/40 while independent revalidation refused all six affected packages.
  The results establish properties of a controlled governance mechanism, not an enterprise invalidation rate, unlearning result, operationally optimal workflow, or measured total-cost reduction.

</details>


### [4] [Developing a Unified Verification and Validation Activity Standard at JPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28600)
*Maximilian Vierlboeck,Ellen Van Wyk,Natalia Sanchez,Bogdan Oaida,Torrance James Eberhart,Marie Piette Gomez,Christopher Delp,Marijke G. Jorritsma,Alex Desharnais*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对NASA JPL验证与确认（V&V）实践碎片化问题，提出统一的V&V活动模式（schema）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年JPL的V&V实践出现分化，导致开销上升、跨项目效率下降、机构知识转移受阻，因此需要统一化的V&V框架。

Method: 通过以人为中心的设计工作坊，汇集29名多任务类型与多学科实践者，构建基于关系的架构，将方法（Test、Analysis、Inspection、Demonstration、Review of Design）分离同时保留公共属性集；以平台无关的SysML模型形式化，定义需求、V&V活动、场所与证据之间的双向关系，并在JPL的Jama平台通过模板与模块化条目类型实现受控定制。

Result: 在Jama平台落地实现，支持受控定制，在严谨性与敏捷性之间取得平衡，并支持自动化、模式复用与数字线程集成。

Conclusion: 统一V&V活动模式可缓解JPL V&V实践的碎片化，提升跨项目效率与知识转移能力，并为自动化与数字线程集成奠定基础。

Abstract: Verification and validation practices (V&V) at NASA's Jet Propulsion Laboratory (JPL) have diverged over the past decade, creating fragmentation that increases overhead, reduces cross-project efficiencies, and inhibits institutional knowledge transfer. We present a unified V&V activity schema developed through human-centered design workshops involving 29 practitioners across multiple mission types and disciplines. The schema builds on a relationship-based architecture that allows for separating methods (Test, Analysis, Inspection, Demonstration, and Review of Design) while maintaining a common attribute set. Formalized as a platform-agnostic SysML model, the schema defines bidirectional relationships between requirements, V&V activities, venues, and evidence. Implementation in JPL's Jama platform demonstrates controlled customization through templates and modular item types, balancing rigor with agility while enabling automations, pattern reuse, and digital thread integration.

</details>


### [5] [Soundness Checking of Taint Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28750)
*Samarth Kishor,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"猜测-验证"方法：用LLM生成方法的精确污点流模型，再用符号算法验证其可靠性。算法推导模型可靠所必须不发生的污点流，并用轻量级静态分析证明；不足时推导最大化通用的被调方法模型并递归验证。在6个大型Go代码库的97个LLM生成模型上，93%被证明可靠，且未引入新的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的静态污点流分析依赖用户手工提供的库方法污点流模型才能扩展到大型应用，但手工精确建模繁琐且可能不可靠；而通过过程间污点分析自动建模又效率低下。因此需要一种既能自动生成可靠污点流模型、又能高效验证其可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出猜测-验证（guess-and-check）方法，包含两部分：(1) LLM智能体生成方法的精确污点流模型；(2) 符号算法验证该模型的可靠性。算法推导为使模型可靠所必须不发生的污点流（must-not-flows），并利用轻量级静态分析（如类型系统和指针分析）证明这些must-not-flows。当这些分析不足时，算法推导最大化通用的被调方法模型并递归验证其可靠性，从而在多数情况下避免完整的过程间污点分析。模型越精确，需要验证的must-not-flows越少，因此LLM模型的精度直接决定方法效率。

Result: 在6个大型Go代码库中，对97个LLM生成的污点流模型进行评估，成功证明其中93%所覆盖方法的模型是可靠的。这些被证明可靠的LLM生成模型同时具有高精度，在证明污点流属性时未引入新的误报。

Conclusion: 该猜测-验证方法能有效结合LLM生成能力与符号验证，为库方法自动生成既可靠又精确的污点流模型，在保持分析精度的同时避免了昂贵的过程间分析，具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Existing state-of-the-art static taint flow analyses for imperative programming languages can scale to large applications by using precise user-provided taint flow models of library methods. However, manually and precisely modeling a method's taint flows is tedious and potentially unsound. Furthermore, automatically modeling the method via an inter- procedural taint analysis can be inefficient. To solve this problem, we propose a guess-and-check approach: (1) an LLM agent that generates a precise taint flow model of a method and (2) a symbolic algorithm to check the soundness of the model. The algorithm deduces which taint flows must not occur in the method for the LLM's taint flow model to be sound, and uses lightweight static analyses (e.g., type system and pointer analysis) to prove these must-not-flows. When these analyses are insufficient, the algorithm deduces maximally-general callee models and recursively verifies their soundness, avoiding a full inter-procedural taint analysis in most cases. Since a more precise model requires fewer must-not-flows to be verified, the precision of the LLM's model directly determines the efficiency of our approach. We evaluate our approach on 97 LLM-generated taint flow models for methods in 6 large Go codebases and prove the models sound for 93% of the methods they cover. The proven-sound LLM-generated models are also precise, resulting in no new false-positives when proving taint flow properties.

</details>


### [6] [Category-Based MLM: Unifying Powertypes with Superclasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28810)
*Mira Balaban,Azzam Maraee,Arnon Sturm*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Category-Based MLM (CatMLM)模型，通过区分不参与深度刻画的category特征与参与深度刻画的object特征，使category类兼具父类（superclass）与幂类型（powertype）双重面向，从而澄清多层建模中的层级语义并给出子类关系与instance-of关系的判定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层级软件建模（MLM）认为广泛领域的建模需要多个分类层级的抽象，其依据来自哲学论证：忠实建模真实领域涉及像自然种类本体那样的重复类型分类。但现有MLM存在核心张力——instance-of本表示非传递的成员关系，而深度刻画（category类影响下层类）却是superclass的本质特征，与之矛盾。因此需要一种界定清晰、可量化的建模框架。

Method: 提出CatMLM模型：category类具有双重面向，即superclass面向与powertype面向；关键依据是将category特征（不参与深度刻画）与object特征（参与深度刻画）加以区分。基于此区分澄清层级（level）的角色，提供可量化的层级判定准则，并导出在subclass关系与instance-of关系之间取舍的决策规则。

Result: CatMLM给出了一个定义良好的MLM模型，具备：(1)基于简单、可量化层级决策的建模机制；(2)层级如何从领域需求中涌现的澄清；(3)对MLM相对于普通OO（plain OO）建模的收益与损失的分析。

Conclusion: 该模型的贡献在于以category/object特征区分为基础，既保留了深度刻画能力，又消解了instance-of非传递语义与superclass语义之间的矛盾，并为多层建模中的层级划分与关系选择提供了明确的判定依据，同时系统分析了MLM与普通面向对象建模的利弊。

Abstract: MultiLevel software Modeling (MLM) suggests that conceptual modeling in broad subject domains might require abstraction of multiple classification levels. The MLM approach relies on philosophical arguments, claiming that faithful modeling of real-world domains involves repeated type classification as in ontologies of natural kinds. MLM leveled architecture is interwoven and defined by instance-of interlevel relationships between clabject classes in lower levels to classes termed category classes, in upper levels. The instance-of relation denotes membership of clabjects as type objects in their (powertypes) category classes, and is not transitive.
  All MLM approaches support forms of deep characterization, i.e., category classes can influence classes in lower levels. Deep characterization is an essential feature of superclasses and contradicts the non-transitive membership meaning of instance-of.
  In this paper, we introduce the Category-Based MLM (CatMLM) model, in which category classes have dual superclass and powertype facets, based on the distinction between category features that do not participate in deep characterization, and object features that do. This distinction clarifies the role of levels, provides a clear quantifiable criterion for leveling, and yields a decision rule between the subclass and instance-of relations.
  The contribution of this paper is to introduce a well-defined MLM model that (1) is based on simple, quantifiable level decisions; (2) clarifies how leveling emerges from domain needs; and (3) analyzes gains and losses of MLM vs. plain OO modeling.

</details>


### [7] [Specification-Driven Benchmarking for Automated Program Repair From Static Corpora to Executable Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28896)
*Yasser Ebrahim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出"规范驱动的基准测试"新范式：基准不再作为静态数据集，而由可执行规范定义并通过生成流水线实例化，实现可控、可再生成、可独立验证的APR评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动程序修复（APR）基准以静态数据集形式构建，其特性继承自所含缺陷数据。该范式虽推动了数十年进展，但有限语料库存在三重局限：实验控制能力有限；随重复使用而日益易受数据污染；无法随评测需求演进而系统性地再生成或调整。

Method: 提出规范驱动的基准测试范式：基准由可执行规范定义，并经基准生成实现。规范显式声明基准的预期属性，包括程序上下文、缺陷分类体系、难度、验证策略与语料库约束；生成流水线通过相互独立的生成、验证与语料管理组件实现这些要求。方法上构建了基准规范维度的分类体系，建立各规范维度到确定性架构职责的映射，并论证独立验证是可信基准生成的结构性必要条件。

Result: 通过一个端到端示例展示规范选择如何沿流水线传播，从而生成属性可独立验证的基准实例，说明规范各维度与架构职责之间的映射关系可被实际落地。

Conclusion: 将基准视为可执行规范而非静态数据集，使基准构建从"工件策管"转向"声明式实验设计"，为APR评测提供可控、可再生成、抗污染且可独立验证的基础设施。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) benchmarks have traditionally been constructed as static datasets whose characteristics are inherited from the defects they contain. While this paradigm has enabled decades of progress, finite corpora provide limited experimental control, become increasingly susceptible to contamination as they are reused, and cannot be systematically regenerated or adapted as evaluation requirements evolve. We propose specification-driven benchmarking, a paradigm in which benchmarks are defined by executable specifications and realized through benchmark generation. The specification explicitly declares the intended properties of the benchmark (including program context, fault taxonomy, difficulty, validation strategy, and corpus constraints) while a generation pipeline realizes those requirements through independent generation, validation, and corpus management components. We develop the conceptual foundations of this approach by introducing a taxonomy of benchmark specification dimensions, establishing how each specification dimension maps to deterministic architectural responsibilities, and arguing that independent validation is a structural requirement for trustworthy benchmark generation. An end-to-end example illustrates how specification choices propagate through the pipeline to produce benchmark instances whose properties are independently verifiable. By treating the benchmark as an executable specification rather than a static dataset, the proposed paradigm shifts benchmark construction from artifact curation to declarative experimental design.

</details>


### [8] [Automatic Harness Evolution for Hardware Design Verification: Can LLMs Consolidate Gains Across Discovered Harnesses?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28908)
*Kidus Seyoum,Ajay Mittur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在硬件设计验证任务中，围绕固定主体模型自动演化智能体外壳（harness）的可行性与局限。结果表明演化能显著提升尝试数量与任务覆盖率，但正确率提升有限，且收益难以跨任务与基准稳定持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体行为依赖语言模型外部的harness，但语言模型能否可靠地改进用于硬件设计任务的harness尚不清楚，因此需要系统研究自动harness演化在硬件设计验证中的实际效果与稳定性。

Method: 在一个固定的主体模型上，针对12个专有设计验证根因定位任务进行自动harness演化；每个任务进行五次试验。另开展CVDP跨基准案例研究，自动演化出一个定宽修复harness，并在142任务参考基线上评估其功能通过数，其中最终功能验证器在修复阶段对主体智能体不可见。

Result: 演化harness将完成的尝试增加71-76%，any-hit任务覆盖率增加80-100%，但总正确尝试仅提升18-24%；可至少复现两次的最强成功仅增加一个任务；候选方案多在任务间交换增益而非累积。辅助候选在排除搜索的四任务验证集上改进，但在含搜索与验证任务的12任务回放中与基线持平。CVDP案例中演化harness比参考基线多出35.6%的功能通过。有用行为分散于不同候选，未整合为跨任务跨指标的支配性harness。

Conclusion: 自动harness演化可提升覆盖与尝试量，但正确率增益有限且难以稳定泛化，各候选呈现互补特化而非一致整合。因此当演化产生互补特化而无法稳定整合时，应采用档案感知（archive-aware）的选择策略。

Abstract: Agent behavior depends on the harness surrounding a language model, but it remains unclear whether language models can reliably improve such harnesses for hardware-design tasks. We study automatic harness evolution around a fixed subject model on 12 proprietary design-verification root-cause localization tasks. Across five trials per task, automatically evolved harnesses increased completed attempts by 71-76% and any-hit task coverage by 80-100%, while total correct attempts improved by only 18-24%. The strongest success reproducible at least twice result improved by one task, and later candidates exchanged gains across tasks rather than preserving them. An auxiliary candidate improved on a four-task validation set excluded from search but tied its baseline on a subsequent 12-task replay containing both search and validation tasks, so the selected gain did not persist across the full pool. Across the tested lineage, useful search, evidence, and finalization behaviors appeared in different candidates but did not consistently consolidate into a single harness that dominated across tasks and metrics. In a separate CVDP cross-benchmark case study, an automatically evolved defined-width repair harness produced 35.6% more functional passes than its 142-task reference baseline; the final functional verifier scored completed outputs but was not shown to the subject agent during repair. These results support archive-aware selection when evolution yields complementary specializations without consistent consolidation.

</details>


### [9] [Human-AI Collaboration for Multi-Line Task Adjustment Using Local Large Language Models and a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29061)
*Teng-Hsien Ko,Chin-Te Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究构建了集成"本地大语言模型+数字孪生+人工决策"的多产线任务调整系统，采用"提议-验证-决策"工作流，实现从操作意图到可追溯审查证据的闭环，并在4条虚拟手术器械分拣线上用30条测试记录进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化系统需在任务、设备状态与人员条件变化时保持适应性，同时须为人工复核提供可追溯的证据，现有系统在语义理解、仿真验证与决策留痕方面结合不足。

Method: 提出"提议-验证-决策"（Propose-Verify-Decide）工作流：将操作员意图转化为结构化需求，生成有界候选策略集合，并依次检查语义、仿真执行与运行约束；通过关联记录保持从请求到验证证据与决策的全程可追溯。使用本地大语言模型、数字孪生与人工决策三组件集成，在4条虚拟手术器械分拣线、30条固定测试记录上评估（28条评估工作流，2条评估模型生成）。

Result: 18例工作流达到预期；自主"策略-工作流"成功率为3/10，无效输入正确拒绝率为7/8；通过前序检查、产生完整证据并进入最终工程评审（CP6）的4例全部通过评审；吞吐约束失败策略均被正确阻断；8条仿真证据记录的平均放置验证通过率97.50%；除启动外，首次可审阅响应与仿真验证的平均时间分别为12.94 s与164.39 s。剩余失败涉及语义失真、证据不完整与无效输入漏检。

Conclusion: 结果证明了分阶段筛选与确认在测试场景中的有效性，并展示了可追溯的策略审查工作流；但未确立整体可靠性与长期稳定性，需通过更大规模测试与物理环境评估来检验泛化能力。

Abstract: Automation systems must adapt to changing tasks, equipment states, and staffing conditions while providing evidence for human review. This study presents a multi-line task-adjustment system integrating a local large language model, a digital twin, and human decision-making. A Propose-Verify-Decide workflow translates operator intent into structured requirements, generates a bounded set of candidate strategies, and checks semantics, simulation execution, and operational constraints. Linked records preserve traceability from requests to verification evidence and decisions. Thirty fixed test records were evaluated using four virtual surgical-instrument sorting lines: 28 assessed the workflow and two assessed model generation. Eighteen workflow cases met expectations; autonomous strategy-workflow success was 3/10, and correct rejection of invalid inputs was 7/8. All four cases that passed preceding checks, produced complete evidence, and reached final engineering review (CP6) passed that review. Together with the correct blocking of strategies that failed throughput constraints, this supports the effectiveness of staged screening and confirmation within the tested setting. Mean placement-validation pass rate across eight simulation evidence records was 97.50%. Mean times to the first reviewable response and simulation verification, excluding startup, were 12.94 and 164.39 s, respectively. Remaining failures involved semantic distortion, incomplete evidence, and missed invalid inputs. The results demonstrate a traceable strategy-review workflow, but do not establish overall reliability or long-term stability. Broader testing and physical evaluation are needed to assess generalizability.

</details>


### [10] [Design, development, and preliminary validity and reliability evidence of the Software Engineering Self-Efficacy Scale (SESES)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29068)
*Albert D. Ritzhaupt,Jhonathan Sora-Cardenas,Priya Ganapathy Prasad,Kai A. Hackney,Neha Rani,Pedro Guillermo Feijóo-García*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究设计并开发了软件工程自我效能感量表（SESES），通过EFA验证其五维结构，解释了约57%的变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域缺乏针对性的自我效能感测量工具，需要开发领域特定的量表以准确评估本科计算专业学生的软件工程自我效能感。

Method: 基于软件工程课程指南与自我效能感概念构建五维框架（需求工程、团队协作、软件质量管理、软件设计与架构、敏捷方法），生成87个初始题项，对527名已完成软件工程课程的本科生进行试测，采用主轴因子法和Promax斜交旋转的探索性因子分析检验量表结构。

Result: EFA提取出与理论框架一致的五个内部一致性潜变量，交叉负荷极小，模式矩阵呈简单结构，累计解释约57%的数据变异。

Conclusion: 软件工程自我效能感是一个由五个理论相关但相互区分潜因子构成的多维构念；研究讨论了局限性与适用范围，并强调领域特定测量的必要性。

Abstract: The purpose of this research is to design, develop, implement, and provide preliminary validity and reliability evidence of the Software Engineering Self-Efficacy Scale (SESES). Framed by a conceptual framework using guidance in software engineering curriculum and concepts along with the notion of self-efficacy, we generated an initial item pool of n = 87 items to operationalize and measure software engineering self-efficacy among undergraduate computing students. The conceptual framework traces five dimensions: 1) Requirements Engineering, 2) Teamwork and Collaboration, 3) Software Quality Management, 4) Software Design and Architecture, and 5) Software Agile Methodologies. We pilot tested the SESES with n = 527 undergraduate computing students who had completed a software engineering course in the current semester or a previous academic semester. We employed Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA) with the Principal Axis Factoring method and an oblique (Promax) rotation to examine the underlying structure of the SESES, resulting in the same five internally consistent latent constructs in the conceptual framework with minimal cross-loading and a simple structure in the pattern matrix, explaining approximately 57% of the variability in these data. Our findings suggest that software engineering self-efficacy is a multidimensional construct of five theorized and correlated, yet distinct latent factors. We unpack the limitations and delimitations of the research while exploring undergraduate computing students' software engineering self-efficacy using necessary domain-specific measurements.

</details>


### [11] [VidTutorAssistant: Automating Responses to Programming Tutorial Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29129)
*Ahmad Tayeb,Sonia Haiduc,Mohammad D. Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VidTutorAssistant：面向编程教学视频评论问答的RAG自动回答平台


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程教学视频评论区中大量学习者追问问题，其数量远超创作者可处理的范围，导致学习者难以获得及时澄清与解答。

Method: 构建Web平台，采用检索增强生成（RAG）流程：提取视频字幕并分段、嵌入；将评论分类为问题/非问题；用余弦相似度检索与问题最相关的字幕片段；以检索片段为上下文，用GPT-4生成有依据的回答。

Result: 在来自7,522个Python与Java教程的105,553条评论中选取440条进行验证：编程语言识别准确率0.99；问题/非问题分类准确率0.96；回答正确率98%、完整率99.5%，而原创作者回答分别为89%和90%。

Conclusion: VidTutorAssistant能有效识别语言、筛选问题并生成高质量回答，其正确性与完整性均优于原创作者，可缓解教学视频问答供需失衡问题。

Abstract: Programming tutorial videos on YouTube are an important information resource for software developers and students, and their comment sections have evolved into active spaces where viewers ask follow-up questions. The volume of these questions, however, often exceeds what content creators can address, leaving learners without the clarifications they need. We present VidTutorAssistant, a web platform that automates responses to viewer questions on programming video tutorials. VidTutorAssistant implements a retrieval-augmented generation pipeline that extracts a video's transcript, then segments it and embeds it. It then classifies each viewer comment as being a question or non-question, retrieves the most relevant transcript segments to each identified question via cosine similarity, and then generates an answer to the question using an LLM (GPT-4), while grounding the response using the retrieved transcript segments as context. We validate VidTutorAssistant through a study on a subset of 440 user comments selected from a larger dataset of 105,553 comments extracted from 7,522 Python and Java tutorials. VidTutorAssistant is evaluated on various criteria: a) its ability to identify the programming language in a video, achieving a 0.99 accuracy; b) its ability to classify comments into questions and non-questions, reaching a 0.96 accuracy; and c) its ability to produce correct and complete answers to questions, producing 98% correct and 99.5% complete responses, compared with 89% and 90% for the original creators' answers.

</details>


### [12] [Orchestrating AI-Assisted Code Remediation: Socio-Technical Bottlenecks in a Large Industrial Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29172)
*Andreas Bexell,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma söderberg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过15天工业级C++仓库单案例田野研究，揭示AI辅助大规模代码修复时，瓶颈从编辑转向CI容量、评审投入与变更编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型长期代码库中的代码退化修复成本高，LLM编码助手虽可规模化执行机械性修复，但其对工业工作流的影响尚缺乏研究。

Method: 在闭源工业C++仓库中开展15天探索性单案例田野研究，由一名经验丰富开发者使用命令行AI编码助手进行大规模问题修复；三角互证Gerrit元数据、开发者日志与团队聊天记录，并采用描述性统计与定性编码分析。

Result: AI辅助修复快速产生数百次提交、触及数千行代码，使CI和评审者注意力饱和；朴素的逐文件提交压垮基于提交触发的CI，改用目录级批处理并限制单次变更文件数后吞吐恢复，但仍需显式请求评审、协商可接受的提交粒度，并迭代跟进以解决构建与静态分析失败。

Conclusion: 当机械性编辑变得廉价时，CI容量、评审工作量与变更编排成为主要瓶颈；超大型仓库中可持续的AI辅助修复需有意识地控制提交、评审与CI批处理粒度，并将语义变更集（如“修复所有警告X实例”）视为一等工作单元，以便针对开发者、评审者和CI进行不同切分。

Abstract: Background: Code degradation in large, long-lived codebases is costly to remediate through manual refactoring and opportunistic clean-ups. LLM-based coding assistants can perform mechanical remediation at scale, but their impact on industrial workflows is underexplored. Objective: We investigate how massive AI-assisted code remediation affects build-on-commit continuous integration (CI), code review, and team coordination in a large industrial repository, and which socio-technical bottlenecks constrain such remediation when source editing becomes cheap through AI assistance. Method: We report on a 15-day exploratory single-case field study in which an experienced developer used a command-line AI coding buddy to remediate widespread issues in a closed-source industrial C++ repository. We triangulate Gerrit metadata with a developer diary and team chat, analyzed through descriptive statistics and qualitative coding. Results: AI-assisted remediation rapidly generated hundreds of commits touching thousands of lines, saturating CI and reviewer attention. Naïve per-file commits overloaded build-on-commit CI; Switching to directory-based batching and capping the number of files per change restored throughput, but still required explicit review solicitation, negotiation of acceptable commit granularity, and iterative follow-up to resolve build and static-analysis failures. Conclusion: When mechanical editing is cheap, CI capacity, review effort, and change orchestration become primary bottlenecks. Sustainable AI-assisted remediation in very large repositories requires deliberate control of commit, review, and CI batch granularity and treating semantic change sets, such as ``fix all instances of warning X'', as first-class units of work that can be sliced differently for developers, reviewers, and CI.

</details>


### [13] [HistoRAG: A Citation-Grounded Question Answering Assistant for Teaching with Scanned Local History and Heritage Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29184)
*Hongzhou Duan,Zikun Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 HistoRAG——一个面向地方历史文化资源的问答助手，能从单一区域馆藏中作答并引用卷号与页码，通过多存储路由实现比基线更准确、成本更低的问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师备课时依赖的地方史料难以使用：主要来源是缺少文本层的扫描书籍，辅助记录是电子表格形式的行政目录。通用聊天机器人回答流畅但缺乏可核验来源，而可验证性恰是教师最需要的属性，因此需要一个能引用出处的问答系统。

Method: HistoRAG 用视觉语言模型转录每一页，并基于 token 概率保留行级置信度；在同一馆藏上构建三种存储：混合文本索引、关系型目录数据库、以及仅从实体密集段落中抽取的知识图谱。轻量级路由器将每个问题分发到所需存储，使计数类问题进入数据库、关系类问题进入图谱。

Result: 在覆盖 36 卷扫描资源及同区域遗产目录、共 516 个问题（含统计、事实、时序、多跳类型）的基准上，HistoRAG 的答题正确率高于段落检索基线和基于图谱的检索系统，且每题成本远低于后者。系统部署在聊天界面后，教师可对任一陈述核对其来源页码。

Conclusion: 通过多存储路由与逐页带置信度的转录，HistoRAG 实现了来源可追溯的高性价比问答，验证了在扫描史料场景下以可验证引用为核心的检索增强生成方案的可行性。

Abstract: Teachers who prepare lessons on local history and cultural heritage work from material that is hard to use. The primary sources are scanned books without a text layer, and the supporting records are administrative catalogs released as spreadsheets. A general chatbot answers such questions fluently but without a verifiable source, which is the property a teacher needs most. This paper presents HistoRAG, a question answering assistant that answers from one regional collection and cites a volume and a page for every fact. HistoRAG transcribes each page with a vision language model and keeps a line level confidence from the token probabilities. It builds three stores from the same collection: a hybrid text index, a relational catalog database, and a knowledge graph extracted only from entity dense passages. A lightweight router sends each question to the stores it needs, so that counting questions reach the database and relational questions reach the graph. We build a benchmark of 516 questions over a collection of 36 scanned volumes and the heritage catalogs of the same region, covering statistical, factual, temporal, and multi-hop questions. HistoRAG answers more questions correctly than passage retrieval baselines and than a graph based retrieval system, at a far smaller cost per question. The assistant runs behind a chat interface, so a teacher can check any statement against the page it came from.

</details>


### [14] [On the Impact of Requirement Smells in LLM-Based Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29208)
*Hugo Villamizar,Jannik Fischbach,Mert Şahin,Julian Frattini,Alessio Ferrari,Alexander Korn,Andreas Vogelsang,Daniel Mendez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过复用既有可追溯性研究的数据集与需求异味分类法，向清晰需求中逐步注入语义、句法和词汇异味，评估其对LLM生成代码功能正确性的影响。结果显示异味密度升高通常伴随测试套件正确性下降，但不同异味类别影响相似，且无味需求也可能生成错误代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明需求异味会影响需求与代码间的自动可追溯性，但其对代码生成的影响缺乏实证证据。作者希望填补这一空白，并比较不同软件工程任务对需求质量影响的敏感性差异。

Method: 基于既有可追溯性研究的基准，复用其数据集与需求异味分类法，并扩展至功能正确性评估。基准包含四个应用的需求及对应系统测试；在原本清晰的需求中逐步注入语义、句法和词汇异味，分析其对生成实现的影响。

Result: 异味密度增加通常与基于测试套件的功能正确性下降相关，但无味需求仍可能产生错误代码；不同异味类别的影响相似。

Conclusion: 需求质量在LLM辅助代码生成中很重要，但高质量需求并不保证正确性，结果还取决于LLM等多种因素。与前期工作相比，需求异味的影响具有任务依赖性：对可追溯性影响有限，而代码生成更敏感，这为LLM辅助软件工程中任务相关质量效应的进一步研究提供了动机。

Abstract: Software requirements are typically incorporated into prompts used in LLM-assisted software development. Recent work has shown that requirement smells can affect automated traceability between requirements and code, but empirical evidence on their effects in code generation remains limited. To address this gap, we build upon a prior study on automated traceability by reusing its dataset and requirement smell taxonomy, while extending it to evaluate the functional correctness of LLM-generated code. Using a benchmark consisting of requirements and corresponding system tests for four applications, we progressively introduced semantic, syntactic, and lexical smells into otherwise clear requirements and analyzed their influence on generated implementations. Our results suggest that increasing \textit{smell density} was generally associated with lower test-suite-based functional correctness, although non-smelly requirements could still produce faulty code. We also found that different smell categories had similar effects. These findings provide additional empirical evidence of the importance of requirement quality in LLM-assisted code generation, while showing that high-quality requirements alone do not guarantee correctness, as these depends on several factors, including the LLM. Compared with previous work, our results suggest that the impact of requirement smells depends on the software engineering task: whereas their effects on traceability were modest, code generation appears more sensitive. Overall, this work motivates further investigation into task-dependent quality effects in LLM-assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [15] [Model-Based Retargeting to Many-Core CPS: Simulink-to-OpenCL Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29311)
*Ryuga Chiba,Hiroshi Fujimoto,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种"工作流保持"的Simulink CPS模型向OpenCL众核处理器重定向方法，借助GPU Coder提取数据并行CUDA代码再转译为OpenCL，并在Kalray MPPA Coolidge2上验证Frenet轨迹规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型开发（MBD）与面向信息物理系统（CPS）的先进众核执行平台之间存在软件可移植性鸿沟，直接为新平台手工重写模型成本高、易破坏原有开发工作流。

Method: 面向具有候选级数据并行性的Simulink CPS应用，构建保持工作流的重定向工具链：先用MathWorks GPU Coder从模型中抽取数据并行的CUDA代码，再通过自研框架转译为OpenCL主机端与设备端代码，涉及语法重写、API仿真以及平台相关的参数打包。

Result: 将该工作流部署到计算密集的Frenet坐标系轨迹规划器上，并运行于Kalray MPPA Coolidge2平台，验证了该重定向流程在所评估CPS工作负载与平台上的可行性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，工作流保持的重定向流水线对该类CPS负载与目标平台是可行的，为MBD模型向OpenCL众核平台迁移提供了无需手工重写的自动化路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the software portability gap between Model-Based Development (MBD) and advanced many-core execution for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS). We present a workflow-preserving retargeting approach for Simulink-based CPS applications with candidate-wise data parallelism to OpenCL-based many-core processors. Rather than manually rewriting models for new platforms, our toolchain uses MathWorks GPU Coder to extract data-parallel CUDA code, which is then translated into OpenCL host and device code via a custom framework. The conversion handles syntax rewriting, API emulation, and platform-specific argument packing. We deployed this workflow for a computationally intensive Frenet-frame trajectory planner on the Kalray MPPA Coolidge2. The results demonstrate the feasibility of a workflow-preserving retargeting pipeline for the evaluated CPS workload and platform.

</details>


### [16] [Demystifying Agent Skills for Smart Contract Auditing: Design, Effectiveness, Behavioral Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29454)
*Cuifeng Gao,Juantao Zhong,Jiachi Chen,Shuai Wang,Daoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统收集83个真实智能合约审计技能（skills），在EVMBench上跨7种agent-模型配置评估其设计特征、有效性与对执行轨迹的影响。发现技能多轻量但设计异质、覆盖漏洞类型不均衡，有效性主要取决于模型而非agent框架（Codex/GPT-5.5提升最大），且技能触发是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent（如Claude Code、OpenAI Codex）已超越编码任务成为通用工具，可通过"技能"（封装领域知识、工作流与工具调用指令的可复用产物）增强能力。然而目前对技能如何设计、以及其对agent有效性与行为有何实际影响知之甚少，本文以智能合约安全审计这一agent已展现潜力的领域为切入点填补该空白。

Method: 从真实环境中系统收集83个智能合约审计技能，在EVMBench上对7种agent-模型配置进行评估，从三个维度展开：(i)审计技能的设计特征，包括结构、知识表示、工作流与工具依赖；(ii)其在提升漏洞检测方面的有效性；(iii)其对agent执行轨迹的影响。

Result: 审计技能大多轻量但设计异质，覆盖漏洞类型广泛却不均衡。技能有效性主要由模型决定而非agent框架：Codex/GPT-5.5增益最大，检测得分提升22.8%，捕获奖励提升43.2%。技能触发是关键瓶颈；一旦触发，技能保持共享的六阶段审计工作流，但在不同配置下呈现出差异化的加载模式及对agent行为的不同影响。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了审计技能的设计特征与作用机制，指出模型能力与技能触发是决定技能效果的核心因素，并公开技能语料库与相关产物以支持后续研究。

Abstract: LLM agents, notably Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, are emerging as versatile tools beyond coding agents only. These agents can be enhanced with skills---reusable artifacts that package domain knowledge, workflows, and tool-use instructions. To date, however, little is known about how such skills are designed or how they affect agent effectiveness and behavior in practice. In this paper, we investigate these questions in smart contract security auditing, a domain in which agents have shown substantial promise. We systematically collect 83 smart contract audit skills from the wild and evaluate them on EVMBench across seven agent--model configurations. Our study examines three dimensions: (i) the design characteristics of audit skills, including their structure, knowledge representations, workflows, and tool dependencies; (ii) their effectiveness in improving vulnerability detection; and (iii) their influence on agent execution trajectories. We find that audit skills are mostly lightweight but heterogeneous in design, covering a broad yet imbalanced range of vulnerability types. Their effectiveness is determined primarily by the model rather than the agent harness: Codex/GPT-5.5 achieves the largest gains, improving detection score by 22.8% and captured award by 43.2%. We further find that skill triggering is a key bottleneck. When triggered, skills preserve a shared six-stage audit workflow while exhibiting distinct loading patterns and differential effects on agent behavior across configurations. We release our skill corpus and artifacts to support future research.

</details>


### [17] [Judgment-Centred Software Engineering Education: A Post-Hype Review and Framework for AI-Augmented Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29473)
*Qusay H. Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2023年至2026年9月的GenAI与软件工程教育研究进行整合性综述，提出"理解债"概念与AASEE框架（五级非线性整合、四项证据义务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI已成为软件开发与计算教育的常态组成部分，软件智能体开始跨仓库、命令行、浏览器、测试等工具行动。教育问题已从"是否允许学生生成代码"转为"SE项目能否在培养学生负责任使用AI的同时，保持并评估人的理解"。现有研究分散，缺乏对学习结果条件性、评估与治理需求的系统整合。

Method: 结构化整合性综述，覆盖2023年至2026年9月23日的研究与实践文献，并补充AI素养、技术债与人机协作的既有工作；分析学生求助行为与作者身份、教师评估与政策需求，以及生成物在仓库、团队、架构与部署系统中长期留存所引发的SE风险。

Result: 证据支持有条件结论：GenAI能改善解释、反馈、练习的可及性与短期任务完成度，但学习成效取决于先备知识、支架、验证、任务设计与评估。作者提出"理解债"（comprehension debt）概念，即AI辅助产出速度超过学习者或团队解释、测试、修改和论证软件的能力时所累积的延迟学习与维护成本。

Conclusion: 将AASEE框架细化为五个非线性整合层级与四项横切证据义务——解释（explain）、验证（verify）、修改（modify）、担责（account），并将委托行为与其后果相匹配的证据、治理与恢复机制相联结。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has moved from a disruptive novelty to a recurring part of software-development and computing-education workflows, while software agents are beginning to act across repositories, command lines, browsers, tests, and other tools. The educational problem is no longer whether students should be allowed to generate code, but whether software-engineering (SE) programs can preserve and assess human understanding while preparing students to work responsibly with increasingly capable AI systems. This paper presents a structured integrative review of research and practice from 2023 through 23 September 2026, supplemented by established work on AI literacy, technical debt, and human-AI collaboration. The evidence supports a conditional conclusion: GenAI can improve access to explanations, feedback, practice, and short-term task completion, but learning outcomes depend on prior knowledge, scaffolding, verification, task design, and assessment. We examine student help-seeking and authorship, faculty assessment and policy demands, and the wider SE risks created when generated artifacts persist across repositories, teams, architectures, and deployed systems. We extend the concept of comprehension debt: the deferred learning and maintenance cost that arises when AI-assisted production outpaces a learner's or team's ability to explain, test, modify, and justify the resulting software. We then refine the AI-Augmented Software Engineering Education (AASEE) framework into five non-linear integration levels and four cross-cutting evidence obligations: explain, verify, modify, and account. The framework links delegation to evidence, governance, and recovery mechanisms appropriate to its consequences.

</details>


### [18] [CodeGraph: Open-Taxonomy Knowledge Graph for Source Code with Wikidata Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29474)
*Federico Pennino,Andrea Gurioli,Stefano Zacchiroli,Maurizio Gabbrielli,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套基于代码专用大模型的源代码语义标注流水线，通过三阶段Wikidata链接与质量保证协议，构建了首个大规模开放分类法源代码知识图谱CodeGraph（约1.58亿节点、约10亿条类型化边）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共软件仓库（GitHub、Software Heritage）存储数十亿文件，蕴含算法、范式、设计模式、应用领域等隐性工程知识，但现有工具局限于语法与词元级分析，难以抽取这些语义知识。

Method: 采用代码专用大语言模型构建开放分类法语义标注流水线；实体通过三阶段链接落地Wikidata：确定性SPARQL阶段处理无歧义实体、Deep Research Agent解析长尾残差、层级上卷阶段导入已解析Wikidata标识符的父类闭包；标注结果物化为源代码专用开放分类法知识图谱；并引入校准的质量保证协议，将小型人工金标准集与LLM-as-a-judge过滤相结合以量化标注精度。

Result: 将该流水线应用于Stack-Edu语料的1.67亿文件，构建首个已知大规模源代码开放分类法知识图谱CodeGraph：约1.58亿节点，含约1.45亿文件、约6.3万个抽取概念实体（算法、范式、设计模式、应用领域等）、约1.98万个落地Wikidata实体；约10亿条类型化边，连接文件与概念、概念与Wikidata标识符及其父类，覆盖14种编程语言。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了利用代码专用LLM与分阶段实体链接可实现源代码的开放分类法语义标注，并成功规模化构建CodeGraph，为源代码工程知识的语义检索与推理提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Public software repositories, like GitHub and Software Heritage Archive, store billions of files, yet extracting their implicit engineering knowledge ---i.e., the algorithms they implement, the paradigms they follow, the patterns they instantiate, and the application domains they serve--- remains challenging, as current tools are constrained to syntactic and token-level analysis. We present a pipeline for building an open-taxonomy semantic annotation of source code using a code-specialised Large Language Model. The extracted entities are grounded in Wikidata through a three-stage linking procedure: a deterministic SPARQL stage handles unambiguous entities, a Deep Research Agent resolves the residual long tail, and a hierarchy-rollup stage imports the parent-of closure of each resolved Wikidata identifier. The resulting annotations are materialised as a source-code-specific open-taxonomy knowledge graph. We further introduce a calibrated quality-assurance protocol that quantifies annotation precision by combining a small human gold set with an LLM-as-a-judge filter. We applied our pipeline to the 167 million files of the Stack-Edu corpus, creating the first known large-scale open-taxonomy knowledge graph for source code. Our graph, named CodeGraph, contains approximately 158 million nodes, which include around 145 million files, about 63,000 extracted concept entities (such as algorithms, paradigms, design patterns, and application domains), and roughly 19,800 grounded Wikidata entities. Furthermore, CodeGraph features approximately 1 billion typed edges that connect files to their respective concepts, link these concepts to their grounded Wikidata identifiers, and relate them to their parent categories, covering 14 programming languages.

</details>


### [19] [Object-to-Source Mapping under Optimization: What a Control-Flow Trace Can Show, and Where Source-Level Coverage Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29656)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文界定了控制流trace的"完整性"与"源码级结构覆盖率证据"之间的边界：优化会破坏指令/分支到源码义务的归因，因此需区分trace完整性、源码归因与覆盖率满足，并介绍WO 2026/162831 A1中一种无需写入应用内覆盖率状态的预编译补充机制，可在-O3构建上经ETM trace重建逐实例条件历史与MC/DC判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已验证的trace区间内，控制流trace足以重建机器级执行，但源码级结构覆盖率还额外要求"生成指令与分支结果→源码义务"的可验证归因。编译器优化（条件融合、条件选择/移动替代分支、调试与映射信息退化）会使trace对生成二进制完整，却令条件级源码证据悬置，故需明确这一边界。

Method: 概念区分：将trace完整性、源码归因与覆盖率满足三分；区分"源码到目标代码的变换"与"目标区域归因状态"；说明内部重汇聚（internal reconvergence）何时使聚合分支观测不足、必须依赖有序的逐实例条件求值历史。方案比较：测量较少优化构建、约束代码生成、添加状态写入插桩。提出预编译补充机制（WO 2026/162831 A1），将选定源码区分保留为trace可观测控制流且不写入应用驻留覆盖率状态。附录在真实芯片上对示例做端到端验证。

Result: 在真实硅片上，补充后的-O3构建使每个条件均可单独被trace观测；从捕获的ETM trace重建出逐实例历史与MC/DC判定，并实测了代码体积与周期开销。

Conclusion: 该机制可弥合trace完整性与源码归因之间的缺口，但适用性仍受限于构建特定的保留性、最终映像映射、trace有效性以及探针效应验证。

Abstract: Within a validated interval, control-flow trace can reconstruct machine-level execution, but source-level structural coverage additionally requires a validated attribution from generated instructions and branch outcomes to source obligations. This paper defines that boundary and illustrates it with exact compiler output. Optimization can fuse multiple source conditions into one branch, replace a branch with a conditional select or move, and degrade provenance in debug and mapping information. In such cases, trace may be complete for the generated binary while condition-level source evidence remains unresolved. We therefore separate trace completeness, source attribution, and coverage satisfaction; distinguish source-to-object transformations from object-region attribution states; and explain when internal reconvergence makes aggregate branch observations insufficient and ordered per-instance condition-evaluation histories necessary. We compare measuring a less-optimized build, constraining code generation, and adding state-writing instrumentation. Finally, we describe a pre-compilation supplementation mechanism disclosed in WO 2026/162831 A1 that preserves selected source distinctions as trace-observable control flow without writes to application-resident coverage state. An appendix demonstrates the mechanism end-to-end on real silicon for the worked example: the supplemented -O3 build keeps each condition individually trace-observable, per-instance histories and an MC/DC verdict are reconstructed from a captured ETM trace, and code-size and cycle costs are measured. Its use remains subject to build-specific preservation, final-image mapping, trace-validity, and probe-effect verification.

</details>


### [20] [On the Factors in Quantum Software Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29705)
*Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以McCall质量模型为骨架，提出一个面向量子软件的基础质量模型，扩展质量因素并引入量子特有维度，给出因素—准则映射、候选度量及其使用条件，并说明其在量子软件生命周期中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算发展，对高质量量子软件的需求上升。经典软件质量模型（如McCall模型）虽有借鉴价值，但未显式刻画量子软件的独有属性，如概率性测量结果、依赖后端的行为以及量子—经典混合工作流，因此需要构建适配量子软件的质量模型。

Method: 采用McCall的“因素—准则—度量”结构作为组织骨架；参考代表性经典软件质量模型与标准以扩展质量因素集合，仅在必要处引入量子特有扩展；定义因素集与质量准则，构建核心因素—准则的多对多映射，讨论代表性候选度量及其执行、参考与解释条件，并展示模型在量子软件生命周期中的应用方式。

Result: 提出了一个量子软件基础质量模型，包含扩展后的质量因素、质量准则、因素—准则多对多映射，以及配套的候选度量与使用条件，并给出跨量子软件生命周期的应用示例。模型定位为结构化、评估与沟通质量问题的共同起点，支持按情境进行适配。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为量子软件质量关注的通用起点，但需通过专家反馈、案例研究与实证研究进一步评估和细化，以适配不同量子软件情境。

Abstract: As quantum computing evolves, the demand for high-quality quantum software is increasing. Classical software quality models, such as McCall's model, provide a useful foundation, but they do not explicitly capture distinctive properties of quantum software, including probabilistic measurement outcomes, backend-dependent behavior, and hybrid quantum-classical workflows. This paper proposes a base quality model for quantum software using McCall's factor-criterion-metric structure as the organizing backbone. It broadens the set of quality factors by drawing on representative classical software quality models and standards and introduces quantum-specific extensions only where needed. We define the resulting factor set and quality criteria, provide a many-to-many mapping of core factor-to-criterion relationships, discuss representative candidate metrics together with the execution, reference, and interpretation conditions needed to use them, and illustrate how the resulting model can be applied across the quantum software lifecycle. The model is intended as a common starting point for structuring, assessing, and communicating quality concerns in quantum software while supporting context-specific adaptation. Further expert feedback, case studies, and empirical studies can be used to evaluate and refine the model and its application across different quantum software contexts.

</details>


### [21] [DSpec2Test: Specification-Driven Test Generation in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29713)
*Sofia Vieira Pinto,Álvaro F. Silva,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DSpec2Test 是一个面向 Dafny 的规范驱动测试生成工具，基于 DNF 等价类划分与可选边界值分析，在不依赖实现细节的情况下从形式化规范自动生成测试，在 131 个变异体上取得 93.9% 的变异杀死率，优于现有实现驱动的 Block 模式（82.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证感知语言（如 Dafny）虽可自动验证程序正确性，但测试在测试驱动开发等验证无法覆盖的场景中仍具价值。然而现有 Dafny 测试生成工具均基于实现，限制了其在这些场景中的适用性，因此需要一种不依赖实现细节的规范驱动测试生成方法。

Method: DSpec2Test 扩展 Dafny 的 generate-tests 命令，新增 blackbox 模式：采用析取范式（DNF）等价类划分，并可选启用边界值分析（BVA）；借助 Z3 SMT 求解器合成满足规范导出约束的输入与预期输出，从而从形式化规范自动派生测试而不考虑实现细节。

Result: 在经 MutDafny 变异的 DafnyBench 程序上评估，DSpec2Test 在 131 个变异体数据集上达到 93.9% 的变异杀死率，高于 Block 模式的 82.4%，并额外独家杀死 17 个变异体。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，规范驱动测试是测试 Dafny 程序的一种有效且互补的方法。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, such as Dafny, integrate logical constructs into code and enable automatic verification of program correctness. However, tests remain helpful in scenarios that verification alone does not address (e.g., to support test-driven development). Existing Dafny test generation tools are implementation-based, limiting their applicability in this context. We present DSpec2Test, a specification-driven test generation tool for Dafny that automatically derives tests from formal specifications, without considering implementation details. Our tool extends Dafny's generate-tests command with a new blackbox mode based on Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) equivalence class partitioning and optional Boundary Value Analysis (BVA). DSpec2Test relies on the Z3 SMT solver to synthesize inputs and expected outputs that meet the specification-derived constraints. We evaluate DSpec2Test on programs from DafnyBench mutated using MutDafny and compare it against Dafny's existing implementation-driven Block mode. DSpec2Test achieves a 93.9% mutation kill rate on a dataset of 131 mutants, outperforming Block's 82.4%, and uniquely killing 17 mutants. These results suggest that specification-driven testing is an effective and complementary approach for testing Dafny programs.

</details>


### [22] [Between the Commits: Process, Error, and Claim Reliability in a Wholly AI-Authored Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29744)
*Douglas Leith*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究发布了一个由Claude AI完全独立编写的21,000行Python工具的全开发历史数据集，并配套两个代码溯源工具与三套分类法，用于分析AI自主编码过程中的指令意图、提交来源与响应可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI（Claude）在无人类编写代码或测试的情况下从零构建完整软件工具的能力，并系统刻画人类与AI协作编码的行为模式与可靠性问题。

Method: (i) 构建包含AI全开发历史的数据集（21,000行Python工具，零人类代码/测试）；(ii) 开发两个代码溯源工具；(iii) 提出三套分类法，分别针对指令意图、提交来源与响应可靠性；(iv) 应用上述工具与分类法对数据集进行分析。

Result: (i) 用户编码代理CLI指令与IDE聊天指令类型不同，更侧重理解、规划与咨询；(ii) 代码开发主要是主动式（proactive）的；(iii) 14.3%的AI代码生成事件包含被AI自编测试套件后续捕获的真实错误；(iv) 约每4-5次AI交互响应中就有1次含一个或多个事实性错误。

Conclusion: AI能独立完成大规模代码开发，但其生成代码与交互响应存在可量化的错误率，提示需借助溯源工具与分类体系对AI编码可靠性进行系统评估与监控。

Abstract: We present: (i) a new dataset consisting of the full development history of a 21,000-line Python tool built entirely by Claude AI, with no human-authored code or tests, (ii) two code-provenance tracing tools, (iii) three taxonomies for instruction intent, commit provenance, and response reliability, (iv) application of these to analyse the dataset. We find that: (i) user coding agent CLI instructions differ in kind from IDE-chat instructions, with a greater focus on comprehension, planning and consultation, (ii) code development is mainly proactive, (iii) 14.3% of AI code-generation events contain a real error later caught by the AI-authored test suite, (iv) roughly 1 in 4-5 of the AI's interactive responses contains one or more factual errors.

</details>


### [23] [SWE-PolyVision: Benchmarking Cross-Image Abductive Reasoning for Repository-Level Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29754)
*Jiajun Wu,Leixin Sun,Zihan Tan,Yitao Liu,Shuo Li,Jiaru Qian,Shanghaoran Quan,Chuangxin Zhao,Yangxu Liao,Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-PolyVision 是一个可执行的仓库级修复基准，通过92个真实任务（含402张静态图像和6个视频）区分多图像证据的“可获得性”与“成功使用”，并在三种访问模式下评估11个编码模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态软件工程基准仅将图像作为额外上下文，未检验智能体能否将分布在不同图像中的证据整合为经验证的仓库级修复，因此无法区分多图像证据的可得性与实际有效利用。

Method: 构建可执行基准SWE-PolyVision：来自36个开源组织的92个真实任务（48个公开、44个私有holdout），含402张静态图像与6个视频，每任务至少两个视觉输入；每个任务将固定修复前仓库与隔离验证器配对；在Text-only、Native Vision、Tool-mediated Vision三种访问模式下评估11个编码模型，并采用与轨迹关联的案例分析及受控干预。

Result: 视觉访问会改变模型所解决的任务集合，但效果同时取决于模型与任务；两个与轨迹关联的Native Vision案例显示互补的视觉与文本线索可导向源码定位且经验证的修复；受控干预表明这种转化在不同输入间尚不稳定。

Conclusion: SWE-PolyVision将多图像证据的可得性与其在仓库级修复中的成功使用相分离，并且不以补丁成功本身作为显式推理的证据。

Abstract: Current multimodal software-engineering benchmarks expose images as additional context, but do not test whether an agent can integrate evidence distributed across images into a verified repository-level repair. We present SWE-PolyVision, an executable benchmark of 92 real tasks from 36 open-source organizations, with 48 public tasks and 44 private holdouts. The release contains 402 static images and 6 videos, with at least two visual inputs per task. Each task pairs a fixed pre-fix repository with an isolated verifier and is evaluated under the supported conditions among three access modes: Text-only, Native Vision, and Tool-mediated Vision. Across eleven coding models, visual access changes which tasks are solved, but effects depend on both model and task. Two trace-linked Native Vision cases illustrate how complementary visual and textual clues can lead to source-localized, verified repairs; controlled interventions show that this conversion is not yet stable across inputs. SWE-PolyVision thus separates the availability of multi-image evidence from its successful use in repository-level repair, without treating patch success alone as proof of explicit reasoning.

</details>


### [24] [Formal Model Construction Guided by Model-Based Proof Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29870)
*Hongshu Wang,Xinyue Zuo,Yufan Cai,Neeraj Kumar Singh,Yamine Ait Ameur,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ProGS方法，以"模型化证明草图"（基于树的证明结构）为核心引导LLM生成与修复形式化模型，将验证失败映射回具体节点/子树实现结构化迭代修复；在27个形式系统的基准上，句法有效性、演绎可验证性与行为正确性均优于现有agentic形式化建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化建模虽能提供强正确性保证，但开发与修复成本高、需要逻辑与形式推理的专业知识。现有基于LLM的自动形式化agent采用"生成-修复"范式，修复由生成模型的验证失败驱动，因而高度依赖反馈粒度与LLM的修复能力；针对某一验证层级的修复可能破坏另一层级的性质，且需要跨完整事件守卫集合进行推理。

Method: ProGS（Proof-Sketch-Guided Formal Model Synthesis）——以模型化证明草图为中心的自动形式化方法。模型化证明草图将目标形式系统的证明结构表示为树：内部节点刻画分支情况（case splits）与归纳推理步骤，叶节点对应实现各子目标的具体状态转移事件。由LLM生成并修复草图，并将验证失败映射到具体节点与子树，为迭代修复提供结构化指引。

Result: 在包含27个形式系统的基准评测中，ProGS在句法有效性、演绎可验证性、行为正确性三方面均优于当前最先进的agentic形式化建模方法。

Conclusion: 围绕层级化证明草图组织形式模型构建具有明确收益，可有效缓解生成-修复范式中修复的盲目性与跨层级回归问题，提升自动形式化的可靠性与正确性。

Abstract: Formal modeling provides strong guarantees about system correctness, but developing and repairing formal models remains labor-intensive and requires substantial expertise in logic and formal reasoning. Recent LLM-based autoformalization agents seek to reduce this burden by generating candidate formal models and revising them using feedback from formal tools. However, the existing approaches follow a generate-and-repair paradigm, in which repairs are driven by verification failures of the generated model and therefore depend heavily on both the granularity of the feedback and the LLM's repair capability. As a consequence, a repair targeting one level of verification may invalidate properties at another level, which requires reasoning over the complete set of event guards. To address these limitations, we propose Proof-Sketch-Guided Formal Model Synthesis (ProGS), an autoformalization method centered on model-based proof sketches. A model-based proof sketch represents the proof structure of the target formal system as a tree. Internal nodes capture case splits and inductive reasoning steps, while leaf nodes correspond to concrete state-transition events that realize individual subgoals. ProGS uses LLMs to generate and repair these sketches, with verification failures mapped back to specific nodes and subtrees to provide structured guidance for iterative repair. Our evaluation on a benchmark of 27 formal systems shows that ProGS improves over state-of-the-art agentic formal modeling approaches in syntactic validity, deductive verifiability, and behavioral correctness, demonstrating the benefit of organizing formal model construction around hierarchical proof sketches.

</details>


### [25] [Era by Eon: Benchmarking Enterprise Agents on Hidden Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30055)
*Benjamin Gruenbaum,Doron Porat,Assaf Natanzon,Roy Zavida,Chen Dinachi,Or Itzahary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对Eon基准区分度不足的问题，新增8类依赖"隐藏事实"的问题模板：答案不由任何文档或题干直接给出，而须由其他数据推断。评估12个智能体（模型+连接企业内部系统的智能体程序）后，最佳智能体在每问三次共24次尝试中答对18次；六个模型中有四个使用任何程序都至多答对6次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Eon基准中题目自带答案规则、可由生成的虚构公司数据直接计算，可运行代码的四个最强模型在27题中各答对22—25题，基准几乎无法区分模型强弱。作者希望构造真正具有区分度的评测：答案所依赖的关键事实被刻意隐藏，任何问题或文档都不陈述该事实，表面包含它的记录显示的是另一回事，只能依靠其他数据交叉推断，例如销售系统称客户因时机放弃购买，而通话录音中客户归咎于服务中断。

Method: 新增八个问题模板，均依赖隐藏事实。对每家生成的公司，由代码填充模板并按无语言模型参与的方式计算出精确答案。评测12个智能体，每个智能体由模型与一个连接公司内部系统的智能体程序配对组成；每题允许三次尝试，共24次尝试。

Result: 最佳智能体在24次尝试中答对18次。六个模型中有四个无论搭配何种程序（agent program）都至多答对6次。最难题目要求从若干相似记录中选出唯一正确项，例如客户签署了三份续约报价中的哪一份；所有智能体在该类两道题上共84次尝试中仅答对1次，即两道题合计仅两次正确。

Conclusion: 通过引入依赖隐藏事实、须跨源推断的题目，Eon基准重新获得了显著的区分度：可运行代码与强模型不再足以应对，多数模型即使借助系统访问程序表现仍接近随机水平。此类"隐性事实+相似记录消歧"的设计能有效暴露智能体在企业真实数据环境下的推理与证据整合短板。

Abstract: In the Era by Eon benchmark, each question states the rules for its answer, and code computes the answer from a generated company's data. When agents can run code, the four strongest models each answer 22 to 25 of 27 such questions, so the benchmark barely separates them.
  We add eight question templates that depend on hidden facts. No question or document states a hidden fact, and the records that seem to hold it show something else. Other data implies it. For example, the sales system says a customer dropped a purchase because of timing. On a recorded call, the customer blames an outage.
  For each generated company, code fills each template and computes an exact answer without a language model. We evaluate 12 agents. Each pairs a model with an agent program, which connects it to the company's systems.
  The best agent answers 18 of its 24 attempts, three per question, correctly. Four of the six models answer at most 6 of 24 with any program. The hardest questions require picking one of several similar records, such as which of three renewal offers a customer signed. All agents together answered two such questions correctly in only 1 of 84 attempts.

</details>


### [26] [Evaluating Agent Skills for Version-Specific Plugin Migration: A Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30120)
*Beiming Liu,Haihao Li,Minjie Chen,Ning Chen,Yiran Wang,Jiming Ye,Puzhao Zhang,Tongtao Wang,Sheng Gao,William Jin,Weihao Mu,Chengzhi Liu,Yucheng Xia,Guangren Wang,Chaoyang Fan,Changfeng Huang,Xunming Lin,Yuanjie Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过64份报告、328条准则判定，追踪一个插件升级技能在16个静态迁移任务上的表现，发现平均奖励从93.83升至98.75，但增益集中于单一任务，且存在利于任一实验组的评分错误；经修正与跨模型复审后结论仍为正但区间跨越零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能虽封装了特定版本迁移知识，但更高的诊断分数并不能直接证明其迁移建议满足目标版本契约，因此需要将聚合奖励与契约层面的证据相连接，检验评估本身是否可靠。

Method: 采用归档式研究：16个静态迁移任务、64份报告、每条件两次尝试、共328条准则判定；将每条判定追溯到其契约域，深度复核10份报告；使用可执行探针验证评分缺陷；替换被复核的判定以做敏感性分析；并由另外两个模型家族的评审在不提供实验组标签与先验分数的条件下重新评分。

Result: 使用技能后平均记录奖励由93.83升至98.75，增益4.92分（95%任务自助区间[0.31, 10.86]），但增益集中于一个任务，且8个任务对被封顶。复核发现双向偏袒的评分错误，例如接受父目录的包含谓词仍获满分，探针证实该缺陷，并显示可用的拆解修复仅因更窄的生命周期评分标准被排除。替换复核判定后估计仍为正（4.61–5.39分），但区间触及或跨越零。跨模型复评与原评审在91.8%和95.7%的判定上一致（加权κ=0.64、0.72），对应增益为10.63和6.09分。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种可追溯的评估方法，将聚合奖励与契约级证据及评审敏感性相连接，并给出针对迁移建议的具体审核检查点；可执行的端到端修复、独立人工标注及其他框架留待未来工作。

Abstract: Agent skills package version-specific maintenance knowledge for coding agents, but a higher diagnostic score does not by itself show that the resulting migration advice satisfies the target version's contract. We study a shipped plugin-upgrade skill through an archive of 64 reports on 16 static migration tasks, with two attempts per condition and 328 criterion decisions. With the skill, mean recorded reward rises from 93.83 to 98.75, a gain of 4.92 points (95% task-bootstrap interval [0.31, 10.86]); the gain is concentrated in one task, and eight task pairs are at the ceiling. Tracing every decision to its contract domain and reviewing ten reports in depth exposes grading errors that favor either arm; in one, a containment predicate that accepts the parent directory still receives full credit. Executable probes confirm this defect and show that a working teardown repair is excluded only by a narrower lifecycle rubric. Replacing the reviewed decisions keeps the estimate positive (4.61 to 5.39 points) but moves its interval to or across zero. Re-grading all 64 reports with judges from two other model families, without arm labels or prior scores, agrees with the original judge on 91.8% and 95.7% of decisions (weighted $κ=0.64$ and $0.72$) and gives gains of 10.63 and 6.09 points. The study contributes a traceable evaluation that connects aggregate reward to contract-level evidence and judge sensitivity, together with concrete review checks for migration advice. Executable end-to-end repairs, independent human annotation, and other frameworks are left to future work.

</details>


### [27] [NEUROTESTGEN: Neuro-Symbolic Guided Test Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30178)
*Ruixin Zhang,Jiho Shin,Hung Viet Pham,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NEUROTESTGEN 是一种结合符号执行与 LLM 的混合测试生成方法，用于按需达成代码覆盖率目标。它先用 Z3 求解器为覆盖目标提取路径约束并生成符号引导规范，再引导 LLM 合成具体测试用例，对复杂对象约束则交由 LLM 推断，并通过迭代反馈循环验证与修正，直至覆盖目标行/分支或达到上限。实验表明其显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化测试生成中，实现高结构覆盖率仍是核心难题：复杂系统的特定行或分支需要满足复杂的控制流与数据流约束。LLM 虽能生成类人测试用例，却难以满足精确的路径条件；符号执行虽可系统推导约束，却难以构造真实可执行用例且受可扩展性限制。

Method: NEUROTESTGEN 采用符号执行与 LLM 驱动测试合成的混合方案：给定方法内的目标语句集合，先由符号分析引擎（Z3 SMT 求解器）提取路径专属约束，构建面向覆盖目标的符号引导规范；再以该规范引导 LLM 合成结构有效、语义合理的具体测试用例。对于涉及复杂对象约束、SMT 求解器难以处理的路径，利用 LLM 推断合理约束。同时引入迭代反馈循环，验证 LLM 生成的测试并提供纠正性引导，直至覆盖目标行/分支或达到限制。

Result: 在广泛使用的基准测试上的实证评估显示，NEUROTESTGEN 在多个 LLM（Llama 3.3 70B、GPT-4o Mini、Claude 3.5 Haiku、Claude Sonnet 4.6）上均显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将符号执行的约束推导能力与 LLM 的测试合成能力互补结合，并辅以迭代反馈验证机制，NEUROTESTGEN 能有效实现按需代码覆盖，在多种 LLM 上超越现有最先进方案，为自动化测试生成中的高覆盖率难题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ensuring high structural coverage remains a fundamental challenge in automated test generation, particularly for complex software systems where reaching specific lines or branches requires satisfying intricate control- and data-flow constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in producing human-like test cases; however, they often struggle to generate inputs that satisfy precise path conditions. Conversely, symbolic execution can systematically derive such constraints, but it often fails to construct realistic, executable test cases and is constrained by scalability limitations.
  In this paper, we introduce NEUROTESTGEN, a hybrid approach that integrates symbolic execution with LLM-driven test synthesis to generate test cases targeting on-demand code coverage. Given a set of target statements within a method, NEUROTESTGEN first employs a symbolic analysis engine (i.e., the Z3 SMT solver) to extract path-specific constraints and construct a symbolic guidance specification for the desired coverage goal. This specification is then used to guide an LLM in synthesizing concrete test cases that are both structurally valid and semantically meaningful. For paths involving complex object-related constraints that are difficult for SMT solvers to handle, NEUROTESTGEN leverages LLMs to infer plausible constraints. Furthermore, NEUROTESTGEN incorporates an iterative feedback loop that validates LLM-generated tests and provides corrective guidance until the target line or branch is covered or a limit is reached. Our empirical evaluation on a widely used benchmark demonstrates that NEUROTESTGEN significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art approach across multiple LLMs, including Llama 3.3 70B1, GPT-4o Mini, Claude 3.5 Haiku3, and Claude Sonnet 4.6.

</details>


### [28] [Jev in the Wild: A Data-Driven Analysis of the Jev Model's Functionality, Applications and Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30216)
*Guoming Ling,Muen Xue,Zijian Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对截至2026年9月22日GitHub上2,170个公开Jev项目进行大规模数据分析，揭示其公共生态的快速增长、跨领域决策用途与公共关注度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev作为快速、低成本的决策模型（支持选择、二元判断与评分）其公共生态迅速扩张，但尚不清楚其在各类应用中如何被使用，以及公共关注度与项目分布之间的关系。

Method: 数据驱动的实证分析：从GitHub采集截至2026年9月22日的2,170个公开Jev项目，统计项目增长、跨领域用途、接口组合方式及公共关注度分布。

Result: Jev公共生态早期增长迅速，既有新项目涌现也集成进已有仓库；项目跨多样领域用于多种决策目的并组合其接口；属性判断与评分使用广泛，而动作选择、内容过滤、模型与工具选择的使用因领域而异。公共关注度集中于路由与接口代理，且与项目数量不相关。

Conclusion: Jev扮演可复用决策组件的角色，其功能随所处工作流而变化；研究提供了Jev新兴生态的定量视图，并为跨应用场景的通用决策模型设计与评估提供依据。

Abstract: Jev is a fast, low-cost decision model that answers natural-language questions with choices, binary judgments, and scores. As its public ecosystem grows rapidly, it remains unclear how Jev is used across applications and how public attention relates to project distribution. To answer these questions, we conduct a large-scale, data-driven analysis of 2,170 publicly available Jev projects collected from GitHub as of September 22, 2026. We find rapid early growth in Jev's public ecosystem, with both new projects and integration into existing repositories. Across diverse domains, projects use Jev for multiple decision purposes and combine its interfaces. Attribute judgment and scoring are widely used, while the use of action selection, content filtering, and model and tool selection varies across domains. These patterns suggest that Jev serves as a reusable decision component whose functionality varies with the surrounding workflow. Meanwhile, public attention is concentrated in routing and interface agents and does not track project counts. Our findings provide a quantitative view of Jev's emerging ecosystem and inform the design and evaluation of general-purpose decision models across diverse application contexts.

</details>


### [29] [Requirement-Bound Verified Commissioning: A Frozen Four-Billion-Parameter Local Model as a Candidate Generator under an External Acceptance Layer with Verification and Release Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30219)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向机电调试中传感器坐标与极性绑定的验收协议：将候选生成与发布权限分离，无解析器支持的需求交由冻结的40亿参数本地语言模型处理，仅当外部闸门在密封语法下能推导出两个事实时才发布方案；协议在预先设定标准下一次性评测144个任务，取得三项贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机电调试中传感器坐标与极性的绑定需要可靠的验收机制，既避免模型幻觉导致的错误发布，又要防止用户错误答案被采纳。作者希望将候选生成与发布决策解耦，用确定性闸门与密封语法约束发布权限，从而在可审计条件下评估该协议。

Method: 协议分离候选生成与发布权限；无确定性解析器支持的需求路由至冻结的本地40亿参数语言模型；仅当外部闸门在密封语法下能推导出两个事实时发布方案；对符合条件的任务向金标准用户索取唯一标准答案。评测按基准构建前固定的标准执行一次，使用144个由隔离智能体上下文编写、无法访问闸门、语法或实验计划的任务。分别测量候选生成与发布决策，并记录路由到不可回答任务、复现发布、假发布及错误用户答案下的发布情况。

Result: 候选人生成与发布决策被分别测量：在22个路由到的不可回答任务中，21个提交了伪造的"就绪"方案，全部被拒绝；同样83次发布无需调用模型即可复现。83次发布中未观察到假发布，在独立同分布假设下得到单侧95% Clopper-Pearson上界0.0354，低于密封的5%阈值；但随后在基准之外seed 0的146次发布中记录到一次假发布。96个可回答任务中仅13个从原文绑定两个事实；其余任务在431组配对中有169次发布了错误答案，包括涉及坐标排除的失败。因资格由答案密钥确定，可部署的提问策略未测试，闸门敏感性与真实用户行为未测量。

Conclusion: 该验收协议实现了候选生成与发布决策的分离及可复现发布，并在基准内满足预设的假发布阈值；但其保护能力有限，基准外出现假发布，且对错误用户答案的防护不足，多数可回答任务未能从原文绑定两个事实。提问策略、闸门敏感性与真实用户行为尚未评估。

Abstract: An acceptance protocol is developed for sensor-coordinate and polarity binding in mechatronic commissioning. Candidate generation is separated from release authority. Requirements unsupported by a deterministic parser are routed to a frozen local language model with four billion parameters. Plans are released only when both facts can be derived by an external gate under a sealed grammar. One canonical answer is requested from a gold-standard user when eligible. The protocol was evaluated once under a criterion fixed before benchmark construction, on 144 tasks written by isolated agent contexts without access to the gate, grammar, or experimental plan. Three contributions are established. First, candidate generation and release decisions were measured separately. Fabricated ready plans were committed on 21 of 22 routed unanswerable tasks, and all were rejected. The same 83 releases were reproduced without model calls. Second, no false release was observed among 83 releases. A one-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson upper bound of 0.0354 was obtained as a diagnostic under an independent-and-identically-distributed assumption, below the sealed 5% threshold. However, one false release was subsequently recorded among 146 releases outside the benchmark at seed 0. Third, protection against incorrect user answers was characterized. Both facts were bound from the original text on 13 of 96 answerable tasks. Incorrect answers were released in 169 of 431 pairings on the remaining tasks, including failures involving coordinate exclusion. A deployable questioning policy was not tested because eligibility was determined from the answer key. Gate sensitivity and real user behavior were not measured.

</details>

### [30] [Learning to Discover Interesting Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28603)
*Niket Patel,Ahmad Rammal,Amaury Hayat,Remi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出以"证明长度与命题长度之比"定义定理的内在趣味性，并训练27B模型预测证明难度，从而生成更有趣、更少与Mathlib重叠的定理，实现自扩展的形式化数学库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能证明越来越多数学定理，但尚不清楚其产生的数学知识是否有趣、有用，即缺乏对定理价值的量化标准与选择机制。作者希望为猜想排序和证明搜索提供可量化信号，以构建无需人工指定目标、可自我扩展的机器验证数学库。

Method: 定义内在趣味性为证明长度与命题长度之比；验证该指标与定理下游效用的外在度量强相关；将给定前提集下证明的难度作为计算这些指标的关键原语，训练一个27B模型来预测证明难度；并据此优化指标，使系统能生成候选定理、筛选最有趣者并迭代构建自扩展数学库。

Result: 所训练的27B模型预测证明难度比前沿通用模型更准确；以该指标优化后模型能产生更有趣的定理，同时将与Mathlib的实质性或完全重叠从91.9%降至30.6%，表明生成了更多分布外数学内容。

Conclusion: 该度量提供了对猜想进行排序和引导形式化数学库中证明搜索的可量化实用信号，为构建不依赖人类给定目标、能自主选择有价值命题的自扩展机器验证数学库提供了路径。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly able to solve advanced mathematical problems, including many that have been open for decades. This opens the door to expansion of mathematical knowledge at unprecedented scale. Yet, while LLMs may be able to conjecture and prove more and more theorems, it remains open whether this new mathematical knowledge is interesting or useful. We define intrinsic interestingness of a theorem as the ratio between the length of its proof and the length of its statement. We show that this correlates strongly with an extrinsic measure of the downstream utility of a theorem. We identify the difficulty of a proof conditioned on a set of premises as a useful primitive for computing these metrics, and train a 27B model that predicts proof difficulty more accurately than frontier general-purpose models. Optimizing for our metric creates a model capable of producing more interesting theorems, while also reducing substantial or full overlap with Mathlib from 91.9% to 30.6%, showcasing the creation of more out-of-distribution math. We show that our system can generate candidate theorems, select the most interesting among them, and iteratively build on a self-expanding mathematical library. These metrics provide a practical and quantifiable signal for ranking conjectures and guiding proof search within formal mathematical libraries. Our framework provides a path towards self-expanding, machine-verified mathematical libraries that can choose worthwhile statements without relying on human-supplied targets.

</details>

### [31] [When Should Forecasting Agents Reason? Behavioral Stress Tests for Reliability Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28475)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在ForecastBench式二元预测任务上，把"检索/推理/服从市场先验/使用历史类比"视为可观测的智能体行为，提出依据可靠性特征进行路由的结构性干预方法ReliabilityRoute。核心结论：机制选择依赖于证据来源，更多推理并非总是更好；2024年拟合的固定规则可逼近人工分类法，而前向自适应规则在16个后续LLM版本上取得最优平均Brier分数，但提升幅度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测智能体日益整合语言模型推理、检索、集成与校准，但"何时该信任哪种行为"缺乏明确依据；需要把机制选择从隐含实现细节转为可观测、可审计的行为，并检验推理是否总是有益。

Method: 在ForecastBench风格二元预测任务上，将检索、推理、服从市场先验、采用历史类比建模为显式行为选择；构建可靠性特征（历史覆盖率、市场先验可得性、来源先验锐度、证据强度、证据分歧度、时间跨度）；设计ReliabilityRoute结构性干预进行路由：一是2024年拟合的固定规则，二是基于已结算历史批次滚动重拟合阈值的前向自适应规则。

Result: 机制选择呈现来源依赖性：结构化类比对部分数据生成过程占优，市场/群体式与保守基线在其他情形更优。固定规则在不硬编码来源名称的情况下接近人工分类法；前向自适应规则在16个后续LLM版本上取得确定性系统中最优平均Brier分数，但增益有限，历史与检索基线仍具很强竞争力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一项行为压力测试：更多推理并不总是更好；预测智能体应先估计哪一证据来源值得被赋权控制，路由策略本身应在可审计约束下自适应演化；复现工件已开源。

Abstract: Forecasting agents increasingly combine language-model reasoning, retrieval, ensembling, and calibration, but it remains unclear when each behavior should be trusted. We study this question on ForecastBench-style binary forecasting tasks, treating the choice to retrieve, reason, defer to a market prior, or use a historical analog as an observable agent behavior rather than a hidden implementation detail. Our central finding is that mechanism choice is source-dependent: structured analogs dominate for some data-generating processes, while market/crowd-style and conservative baselines are better for others. We introduce ReliabilityRoute, a structural intervention that steers forecasting-agent behavior using reliability features such as historical coverage, market-prior availability, source-prior sharpness, evidence strength, evidence disagreement, and horizon. A fixed 2024-fitted rule closely matches a hand taxonomy without hard-coded source-name decisions, while a walk-forward self-adjusting rule refits thresholds from previously resolved vintages and obtains the best mean Brier score among our deterministic systems across 16 later LLM vintages. The gain is modest and historical/search baselines remain highly competitive. The main contribution is therefore a behavioral stress test showing that more reasoning is not always better; forecasting agents should first estimate which evidence source deserves control, routing policies should themselves adapt under auditable constraints, and reproducibility artifacts are available at https://github.com/louiswang524/forcastagent

</details>


### [32] [TW3Cast: A Frozen Router of Lightly Fine-Tuned Foundation Models for Time-Series Forecasting on GIFT-Eval, Selected Entirely on the Training Split](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28506)
*Nathan Thierry,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TW3Cast 是一个不含智能体、不含语言模型的时间序列预测系统，通过一次性在训练集上计算并冻结的路由表，为 97 个数据/频率/预测步长配置分配四种模式之一，在 GIFT-Eval 榜单上以平均 MASE 排名取得 130 个条目中的第 3 名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前排行榜前两名均属依赖智能体或语言模型的 agentic 类别多步系统，本文希望证明不借助智能体与语言模型、仅凭可复现的固定路由表与轻量微调的基础模型，也能接近甚至达到榜单顶尖水平。

Method: TW3Cast 的选择表在训练划分上一次性计算并冻结，其专家为在训练集上轻度微调的公开基础模型。针对 97 个配置，表中提供四种模式：由显式规则清洗与增强训练数据后对 Chronos-2、TiRex 或 Toto 做 LoRA 或全量微调的专家；包含专家的分位数混合；基础模型混合；以及在训练划分切出的回测上进行的选拔锦标赛。所有决策均在回测上做出，专家一旦胜过锦标赛即被纳入，单个候选仅需数 MB 与数分钟 GPU 时间。另设三重防护机制：准确率与校准双重准则、对训练期见过该序列的候选施加非对称边际、以及每窗口的保守门控；选择规则本身亦在时间元回测中确定。

Result: 最佳基础模型单独使用的平均 MASE 排名为 33.8，锦标赛在所有配置上的排名为 38.0，而完整路由器达到 19.4，对应 GIFT-Eval 榜单上 130 个条目中的第 3 名（截至 2026-09-14）。

Conclusion: 通过冻结路由表、轻量微调专家与多重防护机制的组合，TW3Cast 在不使用智能体与语言模型的情况下取得强竞争力表现。路由表、专家索引、固定基础模型版本、提交分数文件及公开分数的时间快照均已发布，论文中所有榜单数字可由单个脚本复现。

Abstract: TW3Cast is a time-series forecasting system that reaches position 3 of 130 entries on the GIFT-Eval benchmark by mean MASE rank, as of 2026-09-14. The two entries above it belong to the leaderboard's agentic category, multi-step systems that use agents or language models to reason about, generate or select forecasts. TW3Cast runs no agent and no language model. Its selection is a table computed once on the training split and then frozen, and its experts are public foundation models lightly fine-tuned on those training splits. For each of the 97 dataset, frequency and horizon configurations, the table serves one of four modes: a specialist, which is a LoRA or full fine-tune of Chronos-2, TiRex or Toto whose training data was cleaned and enriched by explicit rules; a quantile blend that contains a specialist; a blend of base models; or a selection tournament played on a backtest carved from the training split. Every decision in the table was taken on that backtest. A specialist is admitted the moment it beats the tournament there, so a candidate costs a few megabytes and minutes of GPU time, and a failed candidate changes nothing. Three guarded mechanisms protect the selection from its own biases: a dual accuracy and calibration criterion, an asymmetric margin against candidates that saw the series during training, and conservative per-window gates. The selection rules themselves were chosen inside a temporal meta-backtest. The best base model served alone reaches a mean MASE rank of 33.8, the tournament served on every configuration reaches 38.0, and the full router reaches 19.4. The routing table, the expert index, the pinned base-model revisions, the submitted score file and the dated snapshot of the public scores are released, and every leaderboard number in this paper regenerates from them by one script.

</details>


### [33] [PAWS: Policy-driven Agentic World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28547)
*Tiviatis Sim,Jia Hui Woon,Xinming Gao,Chen Gao,Fengbin Zhu,Zheng Huanhuan,Chua Tat Seng,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAWS数据集：覆盖36个美国金融经济政策事件、12,727条政策相关新闻和65,291个有来源依据的利益相关者行动，用于政策驱动的多智能体世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策干预通过公共传播、机构决策与利益相关者响应传导，但现有金融多智能体模拟数据集很少将这些过程与时间对齐的历史证据相连接。

Method: 构建政策驱动数据集PAWS：每个行动关联支撑新闻，并用多层事件框架表示（交互模式、金融行动族与子类型、语义属性、外部分类映射）；实体归一化为机构，行动与日度市场收益对齐；在2,522个分层行动样本上由AI与人类独立标注并裁决分歧；通过2008年卖空禁令与2001年小数化案例及回放研究进行验证。

Result: 交互模式初始一致率达89.4%；案例研究在密集与稀疏新闻条件下均复原了政策时间线与相关市场模式；回放研究表明高准确率可能掩盖对稀有利益相关者行动的漏检。

Conclusion: PAWS为历史锚定的金融模拟提供了可审计基础，可用于评估智能体影响、政策响应级联与行动-结果对齐；行动时序与校准是核心挑战。

Abstract: Policy interventions propagate through public communication, institutional decisions, and stakeholder responses, yet datasets for financial multi-agent simulation rarely connect these processes to temporally aligned historical evidence. We introduce PAWS, a Policy-driven Agentic World Simulation dataset covering 36 verified U.S. financial and economic policy episodes, 12,727 policy-linked news records, and 65,291 source-grounded stakeholder actions. Each action is linked to its supporting news and represented by a multi-layer event frame capturing its interaction mode, financial-action family and subtype, semantic attributes, and conditional mappings to external taxonomies. Entities are resolved to normalized organizations, and actions are aligned with daily market-return context to support policy-agent simulation replay. On 2,522 stratified action samples, independent AI and human reviewers achieved 89.4% initial agreement on interaction mode, with disagreements subsequently adjudicated. Case studies of the 2008 short-selling ban and 2001 decimalization recover documented policy timelines and associated market patterns across both dense and sparse news settings. A replay study further shows that high accuracy can mask failure to detect rare stakeholder actions, identifying action timing and calibration as central challenges.
